"font_size": 13, "horizontal_alignment": "left", "vertical_alignment": "top"}, "value": "1.1080P HD on 5.5” Screen: 1080P HD resolution on a 5.5-inch display lets you see your baby clearly at a glance, whether day or night.\n2.Non-WiFi Stable & Private Connection: FHSS closed-circuit connection delivers a secure, interference-free signal up to 1640 ft (500 m), with no WiFi required for full privacy.\n3.Real-Time Smart Alerts: Detects crying, coughing, and movement, with alerts when your baby leaves a customized safety zone - helping you stay informed and reassured.\n4.5800mAh Long Battery: 5800mAh high-capacity battery supports extended monitoring, including up to 120 hours in ECO mode, reducing the need for frequent charging.\n5. Full Coverage Viewing with Flexible Remote Control: 350° pan-tilt-zoom and digital zoom let you adjust the view remotely and keep your baby in sight as they move.\n "}, {"border_styles": {"bottom": {"color": "rgb(0, 0, 0)", "style": "solid", "weight": "thin"}, "left": {"color": "rgb(0, 0, 0)", "style": "solid", "weight": "thin"}, "right": {"color": "rgb(0, 0, 0)", "style": "solid", "weight": "thin"}, "top": {"color": "rgb(0, 0, 0)", "style": "solid", "weight": "thin"}}, "cell_styles": {"font_color": "rgb(0, 0, 0)", "font_family": "Times New Roman", "font_size": 13, "horizontal_alignment": "left", "vertical_alignment": "top"}, "value": "The Momcozy 5.5 Inch Full HD Smart Non-WiFi Baby Monitor is a video baby monitor designed for parents who want clear, private, and smarter baby viewing without relying on home WiFi. It includes a dedicated parent unit and a 1080P Full HD baby monitor camera, giving families a secure local connection for everyday nursery check-ins, naps, and overnight monitoring.\n \nBM03S combines a 5.5 inch Full HD screen, 1080P camera clarity, and a secure FHSS local connection with smart viewing features that make monitoring easier. Auto face focus helps keep your baby’s face easier to see on screen, while 360° pan, 140° tilt, and up to 4x zoom let you adjust the view as your baby moves. Smart sound, motion, cry, cough, and safe-zone notifications help parents notice meaningful changes without constantly watching the screen. Night view, two-way audio, and ENC noise reduction support clearer check-ins day and night."}, {"border_styles": {"bottom": {"color": "rgb(0, 0, 0)", "style": "solid", "weight": "thin"}, "left":
